```python
"""
Search for low-score non-negative step-function heights.

The routine combines several complementary ideas:
1. re-use the supplied previous construction if available;
2. optimize a small number of piecewise-constant templates to find good basins;
3. local projected/subgradient + multiplicative refinement on the full sequence;
4. occasional first-order LP escape directions;
5. binary-support greedy construction with integer pair-sum balancing;
6. progressive upsampling of the best sequence and final local refinement.

The objective is scale-invariant, so all internal searches work with normalized
sum-1 non-negative sequences and use FFT convolution.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _scipy_linprog
except Exception:
    _scipy_linprog = None


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic sequence utilities
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Normalize a vector to sum 1 and clip it to be non-negative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def conv_fft(a):
    """Full linear convolution a * a using FFT, returned as non-negative float."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    c = c[:need]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_unormalized(a):
    """
    Scale-invariant score equal to evaluate_sequence for valid, capped values:
        2 * n * max(convol(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2)
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def update_state(state, seq, score=None):
    """Update the global best state and print informative progress."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if a.size == 0:
        return False
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = score_unormalized(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return False
    old = float(state.get("score", float("inf")))
    if score < old - 1e-12:
        state["seq"] = a.copy()
        state["score"] = float(score)
        if (not np.isfinite(old)) or (score < old - 0.002) or score < 1.60:
            print(f"new best score={score:.10f} n={a.size}", flush=True)
        return True
    return False


def add_sequence(state, seq):
    """Convenience wrapper: add a sequence without a precomputed score."""
    return update_state(state, seq, None)


def resize_to_length(a, N):
    """Resize a normalized non-negative sequence to exact length N."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    N = int(max(1, N))
    m = a.size
    if m == N:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)

    if m < N:
        # Linear upsampling.
        x = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), N)
        i0 = np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
        i1 = np.minimum(i0 + 1, m - 1)
        frac = x - i0
        out = a[i0] * (1.0 - frac) + a[i1] * frac
        return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(out, 0.0))
    else:
        # Downsample by block sums.
        out = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(N):
            lo = (i * m) // N
            hi = ((i + 1) * m) // N
            if hi > lo:
                out[i] = a[lo:hi].sum()
        return normalize_nonneg(out)


def load_provided_sequence():
    """Load the optional previous construction from height_sequence_1."""
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def random_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.10):
    """Random non-negative perturbation for escaping local minima."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    h = np.maximum(a, 0.0).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
    if mode == 0:
        h *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
    elif mode == 1:
        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 4))):
            wlo = max(1, n // 256)
            whi = max(wlo, n // 16)
            width = int(rng.integers(wlo, whi + 1))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            h[start:start + width] *= np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.2 * sigma)))
    elif mode == 2:
        k = max(1, min(n, int(0.06 * n)))
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.5 * sigma, k))
    else:
        h = a * rng.random(n)
        if h.sum() <= 0.0:
            h = a.copy()
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(h, 0.0))


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initial sequence generators
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_initial(N, kind, rng, best_seq=None, provided_seq=None):
    """
    Create a normalized non-negative initial sequence of length N.
    kind:
      -1 : resize current best
      -2 : resize provided sequence
       0 : Gaussian
       1 : exponential
       2 : Beta-like shape
       3 : adaptive two-block template
       4 : random blocks
       5 : smoothed random
       6 : sine ripple
       7 : three-block template
       8 : power law
       9 : random binary support
      10 : default random
    """
    N = int(max(2, N))

    if kind == -1:
        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size > 0:
            return resize_to_length(best_seq, N)
        kind = 3
    elif kind == -2:
        if provided_seq is not None and provided_seq.size > 0:
            return resize_to_length(provided_seq, N)
        kind = 3

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
    a = None

    if kind == 0:
        mu = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.12, 0.36))
        a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sigma) ** 2)
    elif kind == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.choice([-3.0, -1.8, -0.9, 0.9, 1.8, 3.0]))
        a = np.exp(alpha * x)
    elif kind == 2:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-0.35, 1.4))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-0.35, 1.4))
        xs = np.linspace(1.0 / (N + 1), float(N) / (N + 1), N)
        a = xs ** alpha * (1.0 - xs) ** beta
    elif kind == 3:
        L = int(N * rng.uniform(0.55, 0.85))
        R = N - L
        m1 = float(rng.uniform(0.45, 0.75))
        m2 = 1.0 - m1
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        if L > 0:
            a[:L] = m1 / float(L)
        if R > 0:
            a[L:] = m2 / float(R)
    elif kind == 4:
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
        raw = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))
        lengths = np.floor(raw * N).astype(np.int64)
        lengths = np.maximum(1, lengths)
        while lengths.sum() < N:
            lengths[int(rng.integers(K))] += 1
        while lengths.sum() > N:
            j = int(np.argmax(lengths))
            if lengths[j] > 1:
                lengths[j] -= 1
            else:
                break
        masses = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        pos = 0
        for L, mass in zip(lengths, masses):
            L = int(L)
            if L > 0 and pos + L <= N:
                a[pos:pos + L] = float(mass) / float(L)
                pos += L
            elif L > 0:
                a[pos:N] = float(mass) / float(N - pos)
                break
        if pos < N:
            a[pos:] = 1.0 / float(N - pos)
    elif kind == 5:
        base = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=N)
        wlen = max(1, min(N, 1 + max(1, N // 80)))
        if wlen > 1:
            w = np.ones(wlen, dtype=np.float64)
            w /= w.sum()
            base = np.convolve(base, w, mode="same")
        a = np.exp(0.60 * base)
    elif kind == 6:
        freq = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = 1.0 + 0.95 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    elif kind == 7:
        c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.12))
        c2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.12))
        l1 = int(N * c1)
        l2 = int(N * c2)
        lm = N - l1 - l2
        m1 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.18))
        m2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.18))
        m3 = 1.0 - m1 - m2
        if m3 < 0.35:
            m1 = 0.32
            m2 = 0.32
            m3 = 0.36
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        if l1 > 0:
            a[:l1] = m1 / float(l1)
        if lm > 0:
            a[l1:l1 + lm] = m3 / float(lm)
        if l2 > 0:
            a[l1 + lm:] = m2 / float(l2)
    elif kind == 8:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-2.2, 2.2))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-2.2, 2.2))
        a = (x + 0.05) ** alpha * (1.0 - x + 0.05) ** beta + 0.01
    elif kind == 9:
        m = int(N * rng.uniform(0.20, 0.65))
        idx = rng.choice(N, size=max(1, min(N, m)), replace=False)
        a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        a[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        a = rng.random(N)

    if a is None:
        a = rng.random(N)
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a, 0.0))


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Piecewise-template global search
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def piecewise_seq(params, n, K):
    """Convert piecewise-template parameters to a length-n sequence."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    K = int(max(1, K))
    dims = 2 * K
    if np.size(params) < dims:
        extras = np.random.default_rng(0).normal(0.0, 1.0, dims - np.size(params))
        params = np.concatenate([np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64), extras])
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)[:dims]

    log_len = params[:K]
    log_h = params[K:2 * K]

    z = log_len - float(np.max(log_len))
    ell = np.exp(z)
    ell = n * ell / float(np.sum(ell))

    base = np.floor(ell).astype(np.int64)
    rem = int(n - int(base.sum()))
    if rem > 0:
        frac = ell - base
        order = np.argsort(-frac)
        for idx in order[:rem]:
            base[int(idx)] += 1
    elif rem < 0:
        order = np.argsort(base)
        for idx in order:
            if rem == 0:
                break
            dec = min(int(base[int(idx)]), -rem)
            if dec > 0:
                base[int(idx)] -= dec
                rem += dec

    hu = np.exp(np.clip(log_h - float(np.max(log_h)), -30.0, 30.0))
    seq = np.repeat(hu, base)
    if seq.size != n:
        seq = np.resize(seq, n)
    return normalize_nonneg(seq)


def piecewise_objective(params, n, K):
    """Objective for piecewise template optimization."""
    seq = piecewise_seq(params, n, K)
    sc = score_unormalized(seq)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e100
    return float(sc)


def random_piecewise_params(K, rng):
    """Random initial parameters for a piecewise template."""
    K = int(max(1, K))
    log_len = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, K)
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
    if mode == 0:
        log_h = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, K)
    elif mode == 1:
        log_h = np.linspace(1.0, -1.0, K) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, K)
    elif mode == 2:
        log_h = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, K) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, K)
    elif mode == 3:
        log_h = np.full(K, -0.45, dtype=np.float64)
        log_h[0] = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.2))
        log_h[-1] = float(rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.9))
        if K > 1:
            log_h[1] = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.2))
    else:
        log_h = np.sort(rng.normal(0.0, 0.55, K))[-1::-1]
    return np.concatenate([log_len, log_h])


def run_piecewise_search(n, K, rng, budget, state):
    """Random restarts + coordinate annealing + optional Powell polish."""
    if budget <= 2.0:
        return
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    n = int(max(32, n))
    K = int(max(2, K))
    dim = 2 * K

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        params = random_piecewise_params(K, rng)
        sc = piecewise_objective(params, n, K)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue

        for _ in range(60):
            if time.monotonic() + 0.15 > deadline:
                break
            trial = params.copy()
            step = 0.20 + 0.60 * float(rng.random())
            for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, max(2, dim // 3) + 1))):
                j = int(rng.integers(0, dim))
                trial[j] += float(rng.normal(0.0, step))
            sc2 = piecewise_objective(trial, n, K)
            if np.isfinite(sc2):
                if sc2 < sc:
                    params, sc = trial, sc2
                else:
                    delta = sc2 - sc
                    if delta < 0.08 and float(rng.random()) < np.exp(-delta / 0.10):
                        params, sc = trial, sc2

        seq = piecewise_seq(params, n, K)
        update_state(state, seq, sc)

        if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    piecewise_objective,
                    params,
                    args=(n, K),
                    method="Powell",
                    options={"maxiter": 18, "ftol": 1e-9, "xtol": 1e-7},
                )
                if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun) and res.fun < sc:
                    params = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                    sc = float(res.fun)
                    seq = piecewise_seq(params, n, K)
                    update_state(state, seq, sc)
            except Exception:
                pass


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Gradient / LP helpers for local optimization
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def conv_grad(p, a):
    """
    For weights p[k] on convolution coefficients c[k], return g[i]
    such that derivative of sum_k p[k] c[k] wrt a[i] is 2*g[i].
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    m = p.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], size)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, size)
    idx = (m - 1) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    return np.maximum(np.real(r[idx]), -1e-30)


def build_active_matrix(f, rows):
    """Matrix rows for active convolution sums: A[r,j] = f[rows[r] - j]."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    n = f.size
    A = np.zeros((rows.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(rows):
        k = int(k)
        jlo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        jhi = min(n, k + 1)
        if jhi > jlo:
            A[r, jlo:jhi] = f[k - np.arange(jlo, jhi)]
    return A


def lp_direction(a, c, active_weights, max_rows=80):
    """
    First-order LP direction:
      minimize grad(delta)
      subject to linearized active coefficients not exceeding current max,
                 a + delta >= 0, delta bounded.
    """
    if _scipy_linprog is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.asarray(active_weights, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n < 4 or n > 1600 or c.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.any(p):
        return None

    max_rows = int(max(8, min(max_rows, c.size)))
    if max_rows >= c.size:
        rows = np.arange(c.size, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        rows = np.argpartition(c, -max_rows)[-max_rows:].astype(np.int64)

    try:
        A = build_active_matrix(a, rows)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if A.size == 0 or not np.any(A):
        return None

    g = 2.0 * conv_grad(p, a)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g -= float(g.mean())
    if float(np.max(np.abs(g))) <= 1e-16:
        return None

    low = -a
    high = np.maximum(1e-6, 20.0 * float(np.max(a)))
    slack = 1.05 * float(c.max()) - c[rows]
    slack = np.maximum(slack, 1e-12)
    Aeq = np.ones((1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = _scipy_linprog(
            g,
            A_ub=2.0 * A,
            b_ub=slack,
            A_eq=Aeq,
            b_eq=np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=list(zip(low, high)),
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
        delta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        delta = np.clip(delta, low, high)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) > 1e-14:
            return delta
    return None


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main local optimizer
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_local(a, time_limit, rng, state):
    """
    Local adjustment for a normalized non-negative sequence.
    Uses smoothed-max subgradients, multiplicative updates, subtractive line
    search, random escapes, and occasional LP first-order directions.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1))
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 0.5:
        add_sequence(state, a)
        return

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    c = conv_fft(a)
    cur = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
    best_local = cur
    best_seq = a.copy()
    update_state(state, best_seq, best_local)

    it = 0
    no_imp = 0
    temp_frac = 0.22
    step = 0.14
    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = 0.75

    small_n = n <= 1024
    lp_every = (0 if _scipy_linprog is None or n > 1200 else (55 if n <= 512 else 130))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.25:
        it += 1
        M = float(c.max())
        cur = 2.0 * n * M

        if cur < best_local - 1e-11:
            best_local = cur
            best_seq = a.copy()
            update_state(state, best_seq, best_local)
            no_imp = 0
            temp_frac = max(0.03, temp_frac * 0.94)
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.97)
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if no_imp > (35 if small_n else 60):
            a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.18)
            c = conv_fft(a)
            temp_frac = min(0.65, temp_frac * 1.30)
            step = min(0.45, step * 1.30)
            momentum *= 0.4
            continue

        tol = max(1e-300, temp_frac * M)
        p = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        active = c >= M - tol
        if not np.any(active):
            active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        elif int(active.sum()) < 3:
            k = min(c.size, 5)
            if k >= c.size:
                top = np.arange(c.size)
            else:
                top = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            active[top] = True

        x = c[active] / tol
        x = x - float(np.max(x))
        w = np.exp(np.clip(x, -30.0, 0.0))
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(sw)) or sw <= 1e-18:
            w = np.ones_like(c[active], dtype=np.float64)
            sw = float(w.sum())
        p[active] = w / sw

        g = 2.0 * conv_grad(p, a)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g -= float(g.mean())
        momentum = beta * momentum + (1.0 - beta) * g
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(momentum)))

        if (not np.isfinite(scale)) or scale <= 1e-15:
            a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.08)
            c = conv_fft(a)
            continue

        accepted = False

        # Occasional LP escape direction.
        if lp_every > 0 and (it % lp_every == 0) and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            delta = lp_direction(a, c, p, max_rows=(80 if n <= 512 else 55))
            if delta is not None:
                for t in (0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.50):
                    h = a + t * delta
                    h = normalize_nonneg(h)
                    ch = conv_fft(h)
                    sh = 2.0 * n * float(ch.max())
                    if np.isfinite(sh) and sh < cur - 1e-12:
                        a, c, cur = h, ch, sh
                        if cur < best_local - 1e-11:
                            best_local = cur
                            best_seq = a.copy()
                            update_state(state, best_seq, best_local)
                        accepted = True
                        no_imp = 0
                        break

        if not accepted:
            mult_attempts = 3 if small_n else 2
            s = step
            for _ in range(mult_attempts):
                logh = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-18)) - s * scale * momentum
                logh -= float(np.max(logh))
                logh = np.clip(logh, -50.0, 0.0)
                h = np.exp(logh)
                hs = float(h.sum())
                if hs > 0.0 and np.isfinite(hs):
                    h /= hs
                    ch = conv_fft(h)
                    sh = 2.0 * n * float(ch.max())
                    if np.isfinite(sh) and sh < cur - 1e-12:
                        a, c, cur = h, ch, sh
                        if cur < best_local - 1e-11:
                            best_local = cur
                            best_seq = a.copy()
                            update_state(state, best_seq, best_local)
                        step = min(0.45, max(0.02, s * 1.15))
                        accepted = True
                        no_imp = 0
                        break
                s *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            sub_attempts = 2 if small_n else 1
            s = max(0.02, step * 0.5)
            for _ in range(sub_attempts):
                h = a - s * scale * momentum
                h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
                hs = float(h.sum())
                if hs > 1e-15 and np.isfinite(hs):
                    h /= hs
                    ch = conv_fft(h)
                    sh = 2.0 * n * float(ch.max())
                    if np.isfinite(sh) and sh < cur - 1e-12:
                        a, c, cur = h, ch, sh
                        if cur < best_local - 1e-11:
                            best_local = cur
                            best_seq = a.copy()
                            update_state(state, best_seq, best_local)
                        accepted = True
                        no_imp = 0
                        break
                s *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            # Tiny nudge or random escape.
            h = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.025, n))
            h = normalize_nonneg(h)
            ch = conv_fft(h)
            sh = 2.0 * n * float(ch.max())
            if np.isfinite(sh) and sh < cur - 1e-12:
                a, c, cur = h, ch, sh
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.05)
                c = conv_fft(a)

    c = conv_fft(a)
    final = a.copy()
    update_state(state, final, 2.0 * n * float(c.max()))


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Binary-support greedy construction
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def greedy_support(n, start_frac, target_score, min_frac, batch, time_limit, rng):
    """
    Greedy construction of a binary support with low maximum pair-sum count.
    For equal weights, official score = 2*n*Rmax/|S|^2.
    """
    n = int(max(8, n))
    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None

    deadline = time.monotonic() + time_limit
    start_frac = float(min(1.0, max(0.02, start_frac)))
    min_frac = float(min(start_frac * 0.9, max(0.02, min_frac)))

    S = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    if start_frac >= 0.999:
        S[:] = True
        S_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        m0 = int(max(1, round(n * start_frac)))
        m0 = min(n, m0)
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=m0, replace=False)
        S[idx.astype(np.int64)] = True
        S_idx = idx.astype(np.int64)

    m = int(S_idx.size)
    cnt = conv_fft(S.astype(np.float64))

    best_S = S.copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * n * float(cnt.max()) / max(1.0, float(m * m))

    min_m = int(max(1, min_frac * n))
    batch = int(max(4, min(batch, n)))

    # Removal phase: remove indices contributing to over-threshold sums.
    rem_deadline = time.monotonic() + 0.72 * time_limit
    while time.monotonic() < rem_deadline and m > min_m:
        target = float(target_score) * float(m * m) / (2.0 * n)
        Rmax = float(cnt.max())
        cur_score = 2.0 * n * Rmax / max(1.0, float(m * m))
        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_S = S.copy()

        if Rmax <= target * 1.02:
            break

        d = np.maximum(cnt - target, 0.0)
        if float(d.max()) <= 1e-15:
            break

        g = conv_grad(d, S.astype(np.float64))
        V = 2.0 * g
        idx2 = 2 * np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        valid = idx2 < d.size
        V[valid] -= d[idx2[valid]]
        V[~S] = -1e30
        V = np.nan_to_num(V, nan=0.0, posinf=1e30, neginf=-1e30)

        if float(V.max()) <= 1e-12:
            # Fallback: random removal from current support.
            p = int(S_idx[int(rng.integers(max(1, m)))])
            chosen = np.array([p], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            k = min(batch, m)
            if k >= n:
                chosen = np.array([int(np.argmax(V))], dtype=np.int64)
            else:
                chosen = np.unique(np.argpartition(V, -k)[-k:].astype(np.int64))

        for p in chosen:
            p = int(p)
            if p < 0 or p >= n or not S[p]:
                continue
            if 2 * p < cnt.size:
                cnt[2 * p] -= 1.0
            old_idx = S_idx
            keep = old_idx[old_idx != p]
            sums = p + keep
            valid_s = sums < cnt.size
            if valid_s.any():
                cnt[sums[valid_s]] -= 2.0
            S[p] = False
            S_idx = keep
            m -= 1
            if m <= 0:
                break

        # Re-synchronize exact counts after each batch.
        cnt = conv_fft(S.astype(np.float64))
        cnt = np.maximum(cnt, 0.0)

        Rmax = float(cnt.max())
        cur_score = 2.0 * n * Rmax / max(1.0, float(m * m))
        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_S = S.copy()

    # Add-back phase: increase support size if it lowers the score.
    add_time = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if add_time > 5.0:
        add_batch = int(max(8, batch // 2))
        improved_best = greedy_add_support(best_S, n, target_score, add_time, rng, add_batch)
        if improved_best is not None:
            best_S = improved_best
            best_score = score_unormalized(best_S.astype(np.float64))

    if best_S is None or int(best_S.sum()) == 0:
        # Fallback to a random nontrivial support.
        best_S = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
        m0 = int(max(1, round(0.4 * n)))
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=min(n, m0), replace=False)
        best_S[idx.astype(np.int64)] = True
    return best_S


def greedy_add_support(S, n, target_score, time_limit, rng, batch):
    """
    Given a binary support, try to add elements to lower the equal-weight score.
    Returns the best support found within the time limit.
    """
    if S is None or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return None
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=bool).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = int(S.size)
    if n < 4:
        return S
    S_idx = np.flatnonzero(S).astype(np.int64)
    m = int(S_idx.size)
    if m == 0 or m >= n:
        return S

    cnt = conv_fft(S.astype(np.float64))
    best_S = S.copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * n * float(cnt.max()) / float(m * m)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    batch = int(max(4, min(n, batch)))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        target = float(target_score) * float(m * m) / (2.0 * n)
        dlow = np.maximum(target - cnt, 0.0)
        dhigh = np.maximum(cnt - 0.9 * target, 0.0)

        Sfloat = S.astype(np.float64)
        gadd = conv_grad(dlow, Sfloat)
        ghigh = conv_grad(dhigh, Sfloat)
        score_cand = gadd - 2.0 * ghigh + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-3, n)
        score_cand = np.nan_to_num(score_cand, nan=0.0, posinf=1e30, neginf=-1e30)
        score_cand[S] = -1e30

        zeros = np.flatnonzero(~S).astype(np.int64)
        k = min(batch, int(zeros.size))
        if k <= 0:
            break

        if float(score_cand.max()) <= -1e29:
            chosen = zeros[rng.integers(0, zeros.size, size=k, replace=False)]
        elif k >= n:
            chosen = np.array([int(np.argmax(score_cand))], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            chosen = np.unique(np.argpartition(score_cand, -k)[-k:].astype(np.int64))

        for q in chosen:
            q = int(q)
            if q < 0 or q >= n or S[q]:
                continue
            old_idx = S_idx
            sums = q + old_idx
            valid = sums < cnt.size

            cnt[sums[valid]] += 2.0
            if 2 * q < cnt.size:
                cnt[2 * q] += 1.0
            S[q] = True
            S_idx = np.concatenate((old_idx, np.array([q], dtype=np.int64)))
            m += 1

            cur_score = 2.0 * n * float(cnt.max()) / float(m * m)
            if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_S = S.copy()
            else:
                # Revert.
                cnt[sums[valid]] -= 2.0
                if 2 * q < cnt.size:
                    cnt[2 * q] -= 1.0
                S[q] = False
                S_idx = old_idx
                m -= 1

        cnt = conv_fft(S.astype(np.float64))
        cnt = np.maximum(cnt, 0.0)
        cur_score = 2.0 * n * float(cnt.max()) / max(1.0, float(m * m))
        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_S = S.copy()

    return best_S


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Top-level search driver
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    """
    Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing the evaluation function.
    Uses a mix of template search, local optimization, binary-support greedy,
    and progressive upsampling/refinement.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = time.monotonic() + (budget - 80.0)

    state = {"score": float("inf"), "seq": None}

    # Guaranteed fallbacks.
    add_sequence(state, [0.25, 0.5, 0.25])
    add_sequence(state, [1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0])
    add_sequence(state, np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64))
    add_sequence(state, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))

    provided = load_provided_sequence()
    if provided is not None:
        add_sequence(state, provided)

    def remaining_time():
        return max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())

    # 1) Quick local warm-up from previous / best start.
    if state["seq"] is not None and remaining_time() > 40.0:
        a = state["seq"]
        target_n = int(min(512, max(256, a.size)))
        seq = resize_to_length(a, target_n)
        limit = min(25.0, remaining_time() - 30.0)
        if limit > 3.0:
            optimize_local(seq, limit, rng, state)

    # 2) Piecewise template search for strong basins.
    pw_tasks = [
        (256, 3, 35.0),
        (256, 4, 40.0),
        (512, 3, 45.0),
        (512, 4, 55.0),
        (512, 5, 55.0),
    ]
    for n, K, b in pw_tasks:
        if remaining_time() > 320.0:
            limit = min(b, remaining_time() - 320.0)
            if limit > 4.0:
                run_piecewise_search(n, K, rng, limit, state)

    # 3) Local refinement from varied starts at n=512.
    kinds = [3, 7, 8, 4, 5, 9, 0, 1, -1, -2]
    for kind in kinds:
        if remaining_time() > 430.0:
            seq = make_initial(512, kind, rng, state["seq"], provided)
            limit = min(28.0, remaining_time() - 430.0)
            if limit > 3.0:
                optimize_local(seq, limit, rng, state)

    # 4) Binary-support greedy construction.
    greedy_tasks = [
        (1024, 1.00, 1.55, 0.18, 55.0),
        (1024, 0.75, 1.50, 0.22, 60.0),
        (2048, 0.85, 1.52, 0.20, 70.0),
        (2048, 0.50, 1.42, 0.25, 75.0),
    ]
    for n, frac, target, minf, b in greedy_tasks:
        if remaining_time() > 520.0:
            limit = min(b, remaining_time() - 520.0)
            if limit > 8.0:
                S = greedy_support(n, frac, target, minf, 36, limit, rng)
                if S is not None and int(S.sum()) > 0:
                    add_sequence(state, S.astype(np.float64))
                    a = normalize_nonneg(S.astype(np.float64))
                    if remaining_time() > 380.0:
                        opt_limit = min(45.0, remaining_time() - 380.0)
                        if opt_limit > 5.0:
                            optimize_local(a, opt_limit, rng, state)

    # 5) Progressive upsampling and local refinement of the current best.
    if state["seq"] is None:
        return [float(1.0 / 3.0), float(2.0 / 3.0)]

    max_n = 4096
    cur_n = int(state["seq"].size)
    same_size_no_gain = 0

    while remaining_time() > 60.0:
        if cur_n < max_n:
            next_n = int(min(max_n, max(1024, 2 * cur_n)))
        else:
            next_n = cur_n

        seq = resize_to_length(state["seq"], next_n)
        rem = remaining_time()
        limit = min(130.0, rem * 0.65)
        if limit < 12.0:
            break

        old_score = float(state["score"])
        optimize_local(seq, limit, rng, state)
        new_score = float(state["score"])

        if next_n == cur_n and new_score >= old_score - 1e-9:
            same_size_no_gain += 1
            if same_size_no_gain >= 2:
                break
        else:
            same_size_no_gain = 0

        cur_n = int(state["seq"].size if state["seq"] is not None else next_n)

    # 6) Final short polish if time remains.
    if remaining_time() > 15.0:
        seq = state["seq"]
        limit = min(45.0, remaining_time() - 5.0)
        if limit > 5.0:
            optimize_local(seq, limit, rng, state)

    final_seq = state["seq"] if state["seq"] is not None else np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0])
    final_seq = normalize_nonneg(final_seq)

    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in final_seq]
        official = evaluate_sequence(lst)  # noqa: F821
        if np.isfinite(official):
            print(f"final official score={official:.10f} n={len(lst)}", flush=True)
        else:
            print("final official score is not finite", flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"final official eval skipped: {exc}", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
